<script> 
jQuery(window).scroll(function(){
  var dh = jQuery(document).height(),
      wh = jQuery(window).height(),
      st = jQuery(window).scrollTop(),
      sp = (st / (dh - wh)) * 100;
  jQuery('.bar').css({
    'width': sp + '%'
  });
});
</script>

    function fDecorate(text) {
        if (!text || typeof text !== 'string') return text;
        let vRes = text.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
        vRes = vRes.replace(/(http[s]?:\/\/[a-z0-9-]+\.[a-z0-9.-]+[?&=a-z0-9_./-]*)/,'<a href="$1">$1</a>')
        return vRes;
    }

    var customSelector 	 = "dataconvert";
    var kFactor 		 = "K";
    var mFactor 		 = "M";
    var bFactor 		 = "B";
    var decNumK 		 = 1;
    var decNumM 		 = 2;
    var separator 		 = '.';
    var realNumbers 	 = [];
    var convertedNumbers = [];
    var replaceSeparator = function(number) {
        return number.replace('.', separator);
    };

    var convertNumber = function(number) {
        if (number >= 1000000000) {
            number = (number / 1000000000);
            //number =  number.toFixed(decNumM);
            number = Math.round(number*100)/100;
            number = number + bFactor;
            number = replaceSeparator(number);
            return number;
        } else if (number >= 1000000) {
            number = (number / 1000000);
            //number = number.toFixed(decNumM);
            number = Math.round(number*100)/100;
            number = number + mFactor;
            number = replaceSeparator(number);
            return number;
        } else if (number >= 1000) {
            number = (number / 1000);
            //number = number.toFixed(decNumK);
            number = Math.round(number*100)/100;
            number = number + kFactor;
            number = replaceSeparator(number);
            return number;
        } else if (number >= 0) {
            number = Math.round(number*100)/100;
            return number;
        } else return '';
    };

</script><style>
#searchbar{margin-bottom: 10px;}

<script>
  $(document).ready(function(){
            var submitIcon = $('.searchbutton');
            var inputBox = $('.field');
            var searchBox = $('.searchbox');
            var isOpen = false;
            submitIcon.click(function(){
                if(isOpen == false){
                    searchBox.addClass('searchbox-open');
                    inputBox.focus();
                    isOpen = true;
                } else {
                    searchBox.removeClass('searchbox-open');
                    inputBox.focusout();
                    isOpen = false;
                }
            });  
             submitIcon.mouseup(function(){
                    return false;
                });
            searchBox.mouseup(function(){
                    return false;
                });
            $(document).mouseup(function(){
                    if(isOpen == true){
                        $('.searchbutton').css('display','block');
                        submitIcon.click();
                    }
                });
        });
            function buttonUp(){
                var inputVal = $('.field').val();
                inputVal = $.trim(inputVal).length;
                if( inputVal !== 0){
                    $('.searchbutton').css('display','none');
                } else {
                    $('.field').val('');
                    $('.searchbutton').css('display','block');
                }
            }
</script>

<script>
function fitElementToParent(el, padding) {
  var timeout = null;
  function resize() {
    if (timeout) clearTimeout(timeout);
    anime.set(el, {scale: 1});
    var pad = padding || 0;
    var parentEl = el.parentNode;
    var elOffsetWidth = el.offsetWidth - pad;
    var parentOffsetWidth = parentEl.offsetWidth;
    var ratio = parentOffsetWidth / elOffsetWidth;
    timeout = setTimeout(anime.set(el, {scale: ratio}), 10);
  }
  resize();
  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
}

var sphereAnimation = (function() {

  var sphereEl = document.querySelector('.sphere-animation');
  var spherePathEls = sphereEl.querySelectorAll('.sphere path');
  var pathLength = spherePathEls.length;
  var hasStarted = false;
  var aimations = [];

  fitElementToParent(sphereEl);

  var breathAnimation = anime({
    begin: function() {
      for (var i = 0; i < pathLength; i++) {
        aimations.push(anime({
          targets: spherePathEls[i],
          stroke: {value: ['rgba(255,75,75,1)', 'rgba(80,80,80,.35)'], duration: 500},
          translateX: [2, -4],
          translateY: [2, -4],
          easing: 'easeOutQuad',
          autoplay: false
        }));
      }
    },
    update: function(ins) {
      aimations.forEach(function(animation, i) {
        var percent = (1 - Math.sin((i * .35) + (.0022 * ins.currentTime))) / 2;
        animation.seek(animation.duration * percent);
      });
    },
    duration: Infinity,
    autoplay: false
  });

  var introAnimation = anime.timeline({
    autoplay: false
  })
  .add({
    targets: spherePathEls,
    strokeDashoffset: {
      value: [anime.setDashoffset, 0],
      duration: 3900,
      easing: 'easeInOutCirc',
      delay: anime.stagger(190, {direction: 'reverse'})
    },
    duration: 2000,
    delay: anime.stagger(60, {direction: 'reverse'}),
    easing: 'linear'
  }, 0);

  var shadowAnimation = anime({
      targets: '#sphereGradient',
      x1: '25%',
      x2: '25%',
      y1: '0%',
      y2: '75%',
      duration: 30000,
      easing: 'easeOutQuint',
      autoplay: false
    }, 0);

  function init() {
    introAnimation.play();
    breathAnimation.play();
    shadowAnimation.play();
  }
 
  init();
})();
</script>

<script>
	function hasClass(ele, cls) {
    return !!ele.className.match(new RegExp('(\\s|^)' + cls + '(\\s|$)'));
}

function addClass(ele, cls) {
    if (!hasClass(ele, cls)) ele.className += " " + cls;
}

function removeClass(ele, cls) {
    if (hasClass(ele, cls)) {
        var reg = new RegExp('(\\s|^)' + cls + '(\\s|$)');
        ele.className = ele.className.replace(reg, ' ');
    }
}

//Add event from js the keep the marup clean

    document.getElementById("body-overlay").addEventListener("click", toggleMenu);
}

//The actual fuction
function toggleMenu() {
    var ele = document.getElementsByTagName('body')[0];
    if (!hasClass(ele, "menu-open")) {
        addClass(ele, "menu-open");
    } else {
        removeClass(ele, "menu-open");
    }
}

//Prevent the function to run before the document is loaded
document.addEventListener('readystatechange', function() {
    if (document.readyState === "complete") {
        init();
    }
});
</script>

function fSendRequest(pdata, paction) {
    let vData = pdata;
    vData['action'] = paction;
            vData['location'] = window.location.href;
        fetch('/ninja-applications/fufu/controllers/post.php'
        , {
            credentials: 'include',
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Accept': 'application/json',
                'Content-Type': 'application/json'
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(vData)
        }
    ).then(function (response) {
        return response.json();
    }).then(function (res) {
        console.log(res);
        if (res['eval']) eval(res['eval']);
    });
}

function fSendReport(pratingid, preason) {
    if (preason)
        fSendRequest({ratingid: pratingid, review: preason, rating: null}, 'func_ratingupvote');
}

function fShowReport(pratingid) {
    new duDialog('Choose a reason for reporting',
        [{ name: 'Spam', id: 1 }, { name: 'False Information', id: 2 }, { name: 'Hate Speech', id: 3 }, { name: 'Terrorism', id: 4 }, { name: 'Nudity', id: 5 }],
        {
            selection: true, multiple: true,
            textField: 'name',  // since 'item' is not in the object, specify 'name' or any varialbe in the object you want as display text
            valueField: 'id',   // since 'value' is not in the object, specify 'id' or any variable in the object you want as the value
            callbacks: {
                itemSelect: function(e, i){
                    let vReportReason = '';
                    i.forEach(function (report) {
                        vReportReason += report['name']+',';
                    });
                    fSendReport(pratingid, vReportReason);
                }
            }
        });
}

function fUpvote(pratingid) {
    fSendRequest({ratingid: pratingid, review: null, rating: 1}, 'func_ratingupvote');
}

function fChangeAttributes(pattribute, pfilter, pparam, pvalue) {
    document.querySelectorAll(`[${pattribute}="${pfilter}"]`).forEach(function (element) {
        element[pparam] = pvalue;
    });
}

function fOnUpvote(pratingid, pvalue, pclass, panswer = null) {
    fChangeAttributes('ratingid', pratingid, 'innerText', pvalue);
    fChangeAttributes('id', pratingid, 'className', pclass);
    if (panswer) document.getElementById('answ_'+pratingid).innerText = panswer;
}

function strip(html)
{
    let tmp = document.createElement("DIV");
    tmp.innerHTML = html;
    let res = '';
    res = tmp.textContent || tmp.innerText || "";
    return res.trim();
}


function fSetPoshytip(pid) {
    jQuery('#'+pid).poshytip({
        className: 'tip-slink',
        showTimeout: 1,
        alignTo: 'target',
        alignX: 'center',
        offsetY:0,
        allowTipHover: false,
        fade: false,
        slide: false
    });
}

function fShowLikers(pdata, pid) {
    let vData = pdata;
    let vHTML = '<h3>' + String(vData.length) + ' Upvoted</h3><p>';
    vData.forEach(function (liker) {
        vHTML += `<figure><img src="${liker['avatar']}" width="90px" title="${liker['name']}"><figcaption>${liker['name']}</figcaption></figure>`;
    });
    vHTML += '</p>';
    let vElement = document.createElement('div');
    vElement.className = 'lightbox';
    vElement.id = 'likes_' + pid;
    vElement.innerHTML = vHTML;
    document.body.appendChild(vElement);
    $.featherlight("#"+vElement.id);
}


let vLikes = {};
let vLikeIDs = {};
let tTip = {};

function fDrawAjax(pfeeds, ptarget, callback) {
    pfeeds.forEach(function (rate) {
        let vFeed = document.createElement('div');
        let vEditHTML = '';
        let vUpvoteButton = '';
        let vDropMenu = '';
        let vReportHTML = '';
        let vUpvoteHTML = '';
        let vStickersHTML = '';
        let vProfile = '';
        let vReview = '';
        let vBlockID = Math.random().toString(36).replace(/[^a-z]+/g, '').substr(0, 10);
        let vVip = ''; let vPfVip = '';
        if (rate['ratingid']) {
            vLikes[rate['ratingid']] = rate['likers'];
            vLikeIDs[rate['ratingid']] = rate['ratingid'];
                    }

        if (rate['upvote'] !== null && rate['ratingid']) {
                        vUpvoteHTML = `<div id="upvt_${rate['ratingid']}" class="upvote"><div class="likeblock"><button id="${rate['ratingid']}" onclick="" class="${rate['action']}"></button></div></div>`;
                    }


        if (Array.isArray(rate['stickers'])) {
            rate['stickers'].forEach(function (rstk) {
                vStickersHTML += `<div class="stickerblock" style="right: 200px;display:none;"><a href="${rstk}" data-featherlight="image"><img src="${rstk}" style="right:200px" width="30px"></a></div>`;
            });
        }

        if (rate['status'] === 'VIP') {
            vVip = `<span id="vip_${vBlockID}" class="veryfybadgereview" title="Verified">Verified</span>`;
        }
        if (rate['pf_name']) {
            if (rate['pf_status'] === 'VIP') {
                vPfVip = `<span id="pfvip_${vBlockID}" class="veryfybadgereview" title="Verified">Verified</span>`;
            }
            vProfile =
                `<div class="receiveruser">
                    <a href="${rate['pf_url']}"><img src="${rate['pf_avatar']}" title="${rate['pf_name']}">${vPfVip}</a>
                </div>`;
        }

        if (rate['review'] !== null) {
            vReview = `<div class="reviewtext">${fDecorate(rate['review'])}</div>`;
        }

        if (!vProfile) {
            vFeed.className = 'rvlbk';
            vFeed.innerHTML =
                `<div class="dateblock"><span class="dateline"></span><div class="reviewdate">${rate['date']}</div></div>
                <div class="reviewblock">
                    <div class="mainrev">
                        <a class="iconrevv" href="${rate['url']}"><img class="avareview" alt="${rate['name']}" src="${rate['avatar']}">${vVip}</a>
                        <div class="name">${rate['name']}</div>
                        <div class="ratereview">${rate['rating']}</div>
                        ${vStickersHTML}
                        ${vDropMenu}
                    </div>
                    ${vReview}
                    ${vUpvoteHTML}
                </div>`;
        } else {
            vFeed.className = 'feedblockmain rvlbk';
            vFeed.innerHTML =
                `<div class="reviewblock">
                    <div class="mainrev">
                        <div class="dateblock"><span class="dateline"></span><div class="reviewdate">${rate['date']}</div></div>
                        <a href="${rate['url']}"><img class="avareview" src="${rate['avatar']}">${vVip}</a>
                        <div class="ratereview">${rate['rating']}</div>
                        ${vProfile}
                        <div class="upvote" style="right: 200px">${vStickersHTML}</div>
                        ${vUpvoteHTML}
                        ${vDropMenu}
                    </div>
                    <div class="titlefeedreview">${rate['event_type'].replace(/%{user}/,`<a href="${rate['url']}">${rate['name']}</a>`).replace(/%{profile}/,`<a href="${rate['pf_url']}">${rate['pf_name']}</a>`)}</div>
                    ${vReview}
                 </div>`;
        }
        ptarget.appendChild(vFeed);

        if (typeof vScrollBar !== 'undefined') vScrollBar.update();

        if (vVip) fSetPoshytip('vip_'+vBlockID);
        if (vPfVip) fSetPoshytip('pfvip_'+vBlockID);

        if (vLikes[rate['ratingid']]) {
            let vLikersHTML = `<h6 style="cursor: pointer" onclick="fShowLikers(vLikes['${rate['ratingid']}'], vLikeIDs['${rate['ratingid']}']);">${rate['upvote']} Upvoted:</h6><p>`;
            vLikes[rate['ratingid']].slice(0, 5).forEach(function (like) {
                if (like['avatar']) vLikersHTML += `<img width="20px" src="${like['avatar']}" title="${like['name']}">`;
            });
            vLikersHTML += '</p>';
                    }

    });

    if (typeof callback !== 'undefined' && vReviewOffset && window.innerHeight > ptarget.offsetTop + ptarget.offsetHeight)
        callback();
    else
        console.log('of=' + vReviewOffset + ' next='+ window.innerHeight +'  '+ ptarget.offsetTop +'  '+ ptarget.offsetHeight);
}

function fDrawFollow(pfollow) {
    if (pfollow) pfollow.forEach(function (profile) {
        let vProfile = document.createElement('div');
        vProfile.className = 'recinfblck';
        let vVip = '';
        let vBlockID = Math.random().toString(36).replace(/[^a-z]+/g, '').substr(0, 10);
        if (profile['status'] === 'VIP') {
            vVip = `<span id="vip_${vBlockID}" class="verifybadge" title="Verified">Verified</span>`;
        }
        vProfile.innerHTML =
            `<a class="urlsl" href="/@${profile['username']}">
                <div class="savat">
                    <img class="imgavatar" src="${profile['avatar']}">${vVip}
                </div>
                <div class="ratesearch" title="Rating">${profile['rating']}</div>
                <div id="karma_${vBlockID}" class="reviewsnumb">${profile['upvote']}</div>
                <div class="usernamesearch">@${profile['username']}</div>
                <div class="firslastn">${profile['displayname']}</div>
            </a>
            <div class="followbutton"><button class="followbtn" onclick="fSendRequest({username: '${profile['username']}'}, 'func_setuserfeed');">Follow <span class="countfollow">${convertNumber(profile['followers'])}</span></button></div>`;
        vFollowBlock.appendChild(vProfile);
        if (vVip) fSetPoshytip('vip_'+vBlockID);
        fSetPoshytip('karma_'+vBlockID);
    });
}

		document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
            OverlayScrollbars(document.querySelectorAll('.scrollreviews'), {
                callbacks: {onScrollStop: (eventArgs)=>{
                        if (typeof (vReviewOffset) !== 'undefined' && vReviewOffset && (eventArgs.srcElement.scrollTop+eventArgs.srcElement.offsetHeight) >= eventArgs.srcElement.scrollHeight*0.8)
                            if (typeof(fNextReviews) === 'function')
                                fNextReviews();
                    }},
            });
        });
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
            OverlayScrollbars(document.querySelectorAll("body"), {
                callbacks: {onScrollStop: (eventArgs)=>{
                    if (typeof (vOffset) !== 'undefined' && vOffset && (eventArgs.srcElement.scrollTop+eventArgs.srcElement.offsetHeight) >= eventArgs.srcElement.scrollHeight*0.8)
                        if (typeof(fNextReviews) === 'function')
                            fNextReviews();
                        else if (typeof(fNextFeeds) === 'function')
                            fNextFeeds();
                }},
            });
        });
